Complements `library-audit` Phase 5 (lexical secrets/dangerous shell). Use when: «evaluate skill X», «score this skill», «review SKILL.md», «is this skill production-ready», «evaluate skill for safety», before merging a new skill or after major rewrites. Output: dimension table, top-3 improvements, optional rewritten version, entry in `out/scores/index.jsonl`. ([artemrudenko/skill-governance-toolkit](https://github.com/artemrudenko/skill-governance-toolkit/tree/HEAD/skills/skill-evaluate) · ★1)
